# 测量与验证孤独谱系耗竭

Jane Mantzalas<sup>1</sup> |Amanda L. Richdale <sup>1</sup> |Xia Li <sup>2</sup> |Cheryl Dissanayake <sup>1</sup>

<table><tr><td>摘要研究者已开始探索孤独谱系耗竭的特征与风险因素,但尚缺乏评估工具。本研究以238名孤独谱系成人为样本,全面考察并比较了未发表的27条目AASPIRE孤独谱系耗竭量表(ABM)以及哥本哈根倦怠量表(CBI)的个人量表和工作量表的心理测量学属性,以评估其作为孤独谱系耗竭筛查工具的有效性。探索性因子分析(EFA)揭示了ABM的四因子结构以及CBI个人量表(CBI-P)的两因子结构。通过四个探索性模型检验因子效度和维度,结果表明ABM具有以“孤独谱系耗竭”为核心构念的单维结构,而CBI-P则为包含两个子量表及核心“个人耗竭”构念的多维结构。其他信度和效度指标包括斯皮尔曼相关、方差分析、接收者操作特征、灵敏度、特异度以及组内相关系数(ICC)。ABM和CBI-P与抑郁、焦虑、压力和疲劳呈强相关。出乎意料的是,耗竭测量工具与掩饰及幸福感测量之间的相关性为中等。通过EFA检验了耗竭与抑郁及疲劳之间的潜在重叠,结果支持ABM与抑郁测量的聚合效度,而相关分析和ICC分析则显示出混合结果。我们的结论是:ABM和CBI-P情绪耗竭子量表是有效的孤独谱系耗竭初步筛查工具。需要在更大且更多样化的孤独谱系样本中进行检验,以进一步考察ABM的心理测量学属性,并理解孤独谱系耗竭与抑郁及伪装之间的关系。</td></tr><tr><td>简易摘要早期研究表明,孤独谱系耗竭可能造成严重损害,并对孤独谱系群体的心理健康和幸福感产生负面后果。在孤独谱系社群之外,大多数人并不了解孤独谱系耗竭,因此我们需要为医疗保健提供者和研究者找到识别和测量该状况的方法。本研究测试了两份倦怠问卷,即新的AASPIRE孤独谱系耗竭测量(ABM)和哥本哈根倦怠量表(CBI),以比较它们在238名孤独谱系成人中检测和测量孤独谱系耗竭的能力。我们还检验了ABM是否测量了一种称为“孤独谱系耗竭”的独特状况,还是其症状更适合用抑郁和疲劳测量来描述。我们的研究发现,ABM和CBI-P的“情绪耗竭”子量表均能准确</td></tr></table>

R E S E A R C H A R T I C L E

# Measuring and validating autistic burnout

Jane Mantzalas<sup>1</sup> | Amanda L. Richdale<sup>1</sup> | Xia Li<sup>2</sup> | Cheryl Dissanayake<sup>1</sup>

<sup>1</sup>Olga Tennison Autism Research Centre, La Trobe University, Melbourne, Australia

<sup>2</sup>La Trobe University, Melbourne, Australia

## Correspondence

## Abstract

Amanda L. Richdale, OTARC, School of Psychology and Public Health, La Trobe University, Bundoora, Victoria 3086, Australia. Email: a.richdale@latrobe.edu.au

Researchers have begun to explore the characteristics and risk factors for autistic burnout, but assessment tools are lacking. Our study comprehensively examined and compared the psychometric properties of the unpublished 27-item AASPIRE Autistic Burnout Measure (ABM), and personal and work scales of the Copenhagen Burnout Inventory (CBI) to evaluate their efficacy as screening measures for autistic burnout, with a group of 238 autistic adults. Exploratory factor analyses (EFA) revealed a 4-factor structure for the ABM and a 2-factor structure for the CBI personal scale (CBI-P). Factorial validity and dimensionality were examined with four exploratory models which indicated a unidimensional structure for the ABM with an overarching ‘Autistic Burnout’ construct, and multidimensional CBI-P structure comprising two subscales and overarching ‘Personal Burnout construct. Other reliability and validity indicators included Spearman correlations, analysis of variance, receiver operating characteristics, sensitivity, specificity, and intra-class correlations (ICC). The ABM and CBI-P were strongly correlated with depression, anxiety, stress, and fatigue. Unexpectedly, correlations between the burnout measures and camouflaging, and wellbeing measures were moderate. Potential overlap between burnout and depression and fatigue was examined through EFA, which supported convergent validity of the ABM and depression measure, while correlations and ICC analyses revealed mixed results. We concluded that the ABM and the CBI-P Emotional Exhaustion subscale were valid preliminary screening tools for autistic burnout. Testing with larger and more diverse autistic samples is required to further examine the psychometric properties of the ABM, and to understand the relationships between autistic burnout and depression, and masking.

## Lay Summary

Early research shows that autistic burnout could be very harmful and can have negative consequences for the mental health and wellbeing of autistic people. Outside the autistic community, most people do not know about autistic burnout, so we need to find ways for healthcare providers and researchers to identify and measure the condition. This study tested two burnout questionnaires, the new AAS-PIRE Autistic Burnout Measure (ABM) and the Copenhagen Burnout Inventory (CBI) to compare how well they were able to detect and measure autistic burnout among a group of 238 autistic adults. We also examined whether the ABM was measuring a unique condition called ‘Autistic Burnout’ or if the symptoms were better described by measures of depression and fatigue. Our research found that both the ABM and ‘Emotional Exhaustion’ subscale of the CBI-P accurately 检测到研究参与者中的自评孤独谱系耗竭，但需要在更具多样性的孤独谱系群 体以及那些有较高支持需求的群体中进行更多测试。我们还需要进一步探索孤 独谱系耗竭与抑郁之间的关系。

关键词

孤独谱系，孤独谱系耗竭，孤独谱系耗竭测量，哥本哈根倦怠量表，因子分析，验证

## 引言

在Raymaker等人（2020）首次发表相关研究之前，“孤独谱系耗竭”就已经在孤独谱系群体中被非正式地讨论。随后一项收集2005年至2019年间社交媒体数据的研究发现，早在2008年就有人提及“孤独谱系耗竭”（Mantzalas等人，2021）。尽管如此，相关研究仍处于初期阶段，已有研究表明，孤独谱系耗竭的特征是使人衰弱的心理、身体和情绪疲惫（Arnold等人，2023b；Higgins等人，2021；Mantzalas等人，2021；Raymaker等人，2020），其原因在于孤独谱系群体在日常生活中比非孤独谱系群体面临更多困难（Gillott & Standen，2007；Moseley等人，2021）。例如，孤独谱系与非孤独谱系群体之间的社交沟通可能导致挫折感和失去自主权，进而引发未满足的需求和缺乏适应措施（Donaldson等人，2022；Sarrett，2018）。这种差异被称为“双向同理心问题”（Milton，2012），近期也被称为“观点脱节”（Arnold等人，2023b），指的是孤独谱系与非孤独谱系群体之间对彼此观点理解的不匹配，从而导致沟通不畅以及双方似乎缺乏同理心和同情心。这些相互冲突的视角会影响社交连接，使孤独谱系群体难以建立友谊，进而可能削弱社会支持并加剧孤独感（Ee等人，2019；Mazurek，2014；Moseley等人，2021）。

导致孤独谱系耗竭的其他因素包括压倒性的感官刺激（例如，强光、人群或噪音），这些刺激可能干扰教育参与（Jones 等，2020；Sarrett，2018）、就业（Hayward 等，2019）、出行（Dempsey 等，2021）、进入公共空间（MacLennan 等，2022）以及重要的身心健康医疗（Malik‑Soni 等，2022）。对常规的意外改变、压力性生活事件或过渡期，以及缺乏对自身环境的控制，都会加重孤独谱系群体的应对能力负担，导致心理健康状况不佳（Muniandy 等，2022）研究表明，孤独谱系群体比非孤独谱系群体体验到更高水平的感知压力，这会对其生活质量和独立性产生负面影响（McQuaid 等，2022）。此外，共病状况如述情障碍和较差的内感受，这些状况不成比例地

高发于孤独谱系群体，可能干扰情绪调节和检测身体压力信号（Hassen 等，2022；Kinnaird 等，2019；Shah 等，2016）。这些因素的组合也可能增加个体发生孤独谱系耗竭的风险。

在社会中，孤独谱系群体是一个少数群体（Botha & Frost, 2020），他们面临污名、歧视和负面刻板印象。这些因素可能加剧孤独谱系群体的感知压力（McQuaid等人，2022），并导致使用‘伪装’或掩饰’行为（Han等人，2021；Pearson & Rose，2021）。最近的一项系统综述发现，孤独谱系污名和对社会归属感的需求等社会心理因素是使用掩饰策略的基础（Zhuang等人，2023）。伪装可被描述为使用有意识和无意识的策略来抑制自身的孤独谱系特征，或采用表演性行为以表现得非孤独谱系，例如预先准备社交脚本、抑制调节行为以及保持眼神接触（Cook等人，2021；Pearson & Rose，2021）。尽管伪装与掩饰之间已识别出概念差异（Radulski，2022），但我们在此使用广义术语‘伪装’，因为它反映了孤独谱系群体在孤独谱系耗竭文献中所使用的语言（Arnold等人，2023b；Higgins等人，2021；Mantzalas等人，2021；Raymaker等人，2020）。孤独谱系群体通过伪装来避免排斥和欺凌，并获得否则可能被拒绝的机会和包容（Bernadin等人，2021；Pearson & Rose，2021）。然而，在伪装过程中对自我和他人的持续警惕性监控既费力又令人精疲力尽（Cook等人，2021；Miller等人，2021）。横断面研究已将伪装与许多不良后果联系起来，包括孤独谱系的晚期诊断、漏诊和误诊、心理健康困难（包括自杀意念和行为）、未被识别的支持需求以及身份困惑（Cook等人，2021；Miller等人，2021），尽管这些关系的因果方向尚未得到最终证实。

初步研究表明，伪装及其他压力源可能导致孤独谱系耗竭（Arnold et al., 2023b；Higgins et al., 2021；Mantzalas et al., 2021；Raymaker et al., 2020）。孤独谱系耗竭的后果可能包括功能障碍、脑雾和解离、执行功能与情绪调节能力下降、先前掌握的技能丧失

detected self-reported autistic burnout among the study participants, but more testing is needed with more diverse groups of autistic people, and those with higher support needs. We also need to explore the relationship between autistic burnout and depression further.

K E Y W O R D S

autism, autistic burnout, autistic burnout measure, Copenhagen Burnout Inventory, factor analysis, validation

## INTRODUCTION

‘Autistic burnout’ was discussed informally by autistic people long before the first research was published by Raymaker et al. (2020). A subsequent study which col lected social media data posted between 2005 and 2019 found ‘autistic burnout’ mentioned as early as 2008 (Mantzalas et al., 2021). Despite this, research is in its infancy, with studies indicating that autistic burnout is characterized by debilitating mental, physical, and emo tional exhaustion (Arnold et al., 2023b; Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020), which occurs because autistic people experi ence more difficulties in everyday life than non-autistic people (Gillott & Standen, 2007; Moseley et al., 2021). For example, social communication between autistic and non-autistic people can contribute to frustration, and loss of agency, resulting in unmet needs and a lack of accom modations (Donaldson et al., 2022; Sarrett, 2018). This disparity, known as the ‘double empathy problem (Milton, 2012) and, more recently, ‘perspective discon nect’ (Arnold et al., 2023b), refers to mismatched under standings among autistic and non-autistic people abou each other’s points of view that leads to miscommunica tions and a seeming lack of empathy and compassion from both parties. These conflicting perspectives can affect social connection, making it difficult for autistic people to form friendships which, in turn, can contribute to diminished social support and increased loneliness (Ee et al., 2019; Mazurek, 2014; Moseley et al., 2021).

Other factors that can contribute to autistic burnou include overwhelming sensory stimuli (e.g., bright lights, crowds, or noise) that can interfere with engagement in education (Jones et al., 2020; Sarrett, 2018) and employ ment (Hayward et al., 2019), travel (Dempsey et al., 2021), access to public spaces (MacLennan et al., 2022) and vital physical and mental healthcare (Malik-Soni et al., 2022). Unexpected changes to rou tines, stressful life events or transitions, and a lack of con trol over one’s environment can tax autistic people’s coping abilities contributing to poor mental health (Muniandy et al., 2022). It has been shown that autistic people experience higher levels of perceived stress than non-autistic people, which can negatively impact their quality of life and independence (McQuaid et al., 2022). Furthermore, co-occurring conditions such as alexithy mia and poor interoception, which are disproportionately high among autistic people, can interfere with emotional regulation and detecting bodily signs of stress (Hassen et al., 2022; Kinnaird et al., 2019; Shah et al., 2016). Combinations of these factors may also contribute to an individual’s risk for autistic burnout.

In society, autistic people represent a minority group (Botha & Frost, 2020) who face stigma, discrimination, and negative stereotypes. These factors can contribute to autistic people’s perceived stress (McQuaid et al., 2022) and lead to the use of ‘masking’ or ‘camouflaging behaviors (Han et al., 2021; Pearson & Rose, 2021). A recent systematic review found that psychosocial factors such as autism stigma and a need for social belongingness underlie the use of camouflaging strategies (Zhuang et al., 2023). Masking can be described as the use of con scious and unconscious strategies to suppress one’s autis tic traits or use of performative behaviors to pass as nonautistic such as pre-preparing scripts for social interac tions, restraining self-stimulatory behaviors, and maintaining eye contact (Cook et al., 2021; Pearson & Rose, 2021). While conceptual differences between mask ing and camouflaging have been identified (Radulski, 2022), we use the broad term ‘masking’ here as it reflects the language used by autistic people in the autistic burnout literature (Arnold et al., 2023b; Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020). Autistic people mask to avoid ostracism and bullying and gain access to opportunities and inclusion they might otherwise be denied (Bernadin et al., 2021; Pearson & Rose, 2021). However, the continuous and vigilant monitoring of the self and others during masking is effortful and exhausting (Cook et al., 2021; Miller et al., 2021). Cross-sectional studies have associated masking with many adverse outcomes, including late, missed and misdiagnosis of autism, mental health diffi culties, including suicidal ideation and behavior, uniden tified support needs, and identity confusion (Cook et al., 2021; Miller et al., 2021), although the causal direc tions of these relationships have yet to be conclusively demonstrated.

Preliminary studies suggest that masking and other stressors can lead to autistic burnout (Arnold et al., 2023b; Higgins et al., 2021;Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020). The consequences of autistic burnout can include the inability to function, brain fog and dissociation, reduced executive functioning and emotion regulation, the loss of previously mastered skills

（如驾驶、烹饪）、无法说话或交流、感觉不耐受加重，以及社交与人际退缩（Higgins et al., 2021；Mantzalas et al., 2021；Mantzalas et al., 2022；Raymaker et al., 2020）。孤独谱系耗竭的时期可持续数月甚至数年，并影响那些本已面临更大负面生活经历和不良心理健康风险的孤独谱系群体的心理健康与幸福感（另见Griffiths et al., 2019；Lai et al., 2019）。长期或反复出现的耗竭可能助长失业和教育成就降低，进而影响孤独谱系群体的长期独立性和生活质量（Mantzalas et al., 2021；Raymaker et al., 2020）。尽管已有早期研究（Arnold et al., 2023b；Higginset al., 2021；Mantzalas et al., 2021；Mantzalas etal., 2022；Phung et al., 2021；Raymaker et al.,2020）以及大量非正式证词，但关于孤独谱系耗竭的认知仍然不足，尤其在孤独谱系社区之外。从幼年开始，孤独谱系群体报告称其耗竭症状被忽视、误解、误诊和错误治疗（Phung et al., 2021；Raymaker et al.,2020），以至于孤独谱系群体已将孤独谱系耗竭确定为一项研究优先事项（Raymaker et al., 2020）。

倦怠研究主要关注以人为中心的职业（如社会工作、教学或医学）中的非孤独谱系个体，超过90%的研究使用了马斯勒倦怠量表（MBI；Schaufeli等人，2009）。MBI测量倦怠的三个维度：情绪耗竭、人格解体（犬儒主义）和个人成就感降低（Maslach & Jackson，1981；Schaufeli等人，2009）。关于MBI是否能完全代表倦怠体验存在争议，因为“情绪耗竭”是唯一在跨研究中保持稳定的维度。相比之下，人格解体是最不稳定的维度，且可能是多维度的（Schaufeli等人，1998）MBI的方法论基础受到批评，因为它是在归纳基础上开发的，而非基于理论或临床基础，并且（最初）假设倦怠只发生在面向人的工作中（Bianchi等人，2015）。其他研究者也质疑了某些条目的文化效度（Kristensen等人，2005）。因此，尽管MBI无疑是占主导地位的倦怠问卷，但批评者认为“倦怠就是MBI所测量的，而MBI测量的就是倦怠”（第193页，Kristensen等人，2005）。总体而言，这些问题对MBI的效度提出了一些合理的担忧，特别是作为非组织性倦怠的测量工具。这促使了替代测量工具的发展，以评估其他形式的倦怠，例如父母倦怠（Roskam等人，2018）和运动员倦怠（Raedeke & Smith，2001）。

一种已验证的测量工具，用于捕捉非组织与工作相关倦怠，是哥本哈根倦怠量表（CBI；Kristensen et al., 2005）；它使用三个量表来评估“通用”个人耗竭、工作相关

倦怠，以及客户相关倦怠。最近一项研究改编了CBI，以调查个人和学术倦怠是否预测了孤独谱系和非孤独谱系大学生在新冠疫情期间的辍学（Cage &McManemy, 2022）。该研究首次将CBI用于孤独谱系人群，结果显示，虽然孤独谱系学生报告的个人和学术倦怠率高于非孤独谱系学生，但倦怠仅预测了非孤独谱系学生的辍学。这些结果支持了“孤独谱系耗竭”这一独特形式。

尽管倦怠研究已开展50年，成果丰硕，但倦怠综合征并未被纳入当前《精神障碍诊断与统计手册》（DSM‑5；APA, 2013），尽管其在最新版《国际疾病分类》（ICD‑11；世界卫生组织，2022）中被提及，但附有严格参数。DSM中缺乏正式认可，部分原因可能在于关于倦怠与抑郁是否为不同构念的持续争论。相似的发生学及核心症状重叠等因素，包括快感缺乏、疲劳和退缩，导致了这种不确定性（Bianchi等人，2014；Bianchi等人，2015；Tavella与Parker，2020）。一项荟萃分析考察了来自不同国家职业倦怠研究中的14个样本，结论认为倦怠并非一种独立的状况，而是属于抑郁症状谱系的一部分，因为其核心方面（疲惫）与抑郁的相关性高于与其他倦怠方面的相关性（Bianchi等人，2021；Bianchi与Sowden，2022）。然而，所有纳入研究均使用MBI测量倦怠，由于前述方法论问题，这可能削弱了这些发现的效度。

职业倦怠研究表明，倦怠可能是抑郁的前兆，若倦怠严重则风险增加（Ahola等人，2005）。尽管已有研究提出两者之间存在双向关系，但“从倦怠到抑郁的路径似乎比从抑郁到倦怠的路径更强”（p.109, Ahola与Hakanen，2007）。在所有现有的质性研究中，孤独谱系成人均已区分了抑郁与孤独谱系耗竭，表明两者可能共现，且孤独谱系耗竭可能诱发并加重抑郁（Arnold等人，2023b；Higgins等人，2021；Mantzalas等人，2021；Raymaker等人，2020）。此外，定性研究提示，当孤独谱系耗竭与抑郁同时发生时，后果可能尤为严重，甚至包括自杀意念（Higgins等人，2021；Mantzalas等人，2021；Raymaker等人，2020）。因此，有必要探讨在孤独谱系耗竭与抑郁之间是否存在类似于Ahola与Hakanen（2007）所报告的相互关系。

阐明孤独谱系耗竭与抑郁之间的区别至关重要，否则可能导致误诊和

(e.g., driving, cooking), an inability to speak or communicate, increased sensory intolerance, and social and interpersonal withdrawal (Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2022; Raymaker et al., 2020). Periods of autistic burnout can last months or years and impact the mental health and wellbeing of autistic people who are already at greater risk for nega tive life experiences and poor mental health (see also Griffiths et al., 2019; Lai et al., 2019). Prolonged or repeated episodes of burnout may contribute to unemployment and reduced educational achievement, which can affect the long-term independence and quality of life of autistic people (Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020). Despite early research (Arnold et al., 2023b; Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2022;Phung et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020) and many informal testimonies, awareness about autistic burnout is lacking, especially outside the autistic community. From an early age, autistic people report that their burnout symptoms are dismissed, misunderstood, mis diagnosed, and incorrectly treated (Phung et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020) such that autistic people have identified autistic burnout as a research priority (Raymaker et al., 2020).

Burnout research has primarily focused on non autistic individuals in people-centred professions such as social work, teaching, or medicine, and over 90% of stud ies have used the Maslach Burnout Inventory (MBI; Schaufeli et al., 2009). The MBI measures three facets of burnout: emotional exhaustion, depersonalisation (cyni cism), and reduced personal accomplishment (Maslach & Jackson, 1981; Schaufeli et al., 2009). Conjecture exists whether the MBI fully represents the burnout experience because ‘emotional exhaustion’ is the only facet tha remains stable across studies. In contrast, depersonalisa tion is the least stable and is possibly multidimensiona (Schaufeli et al., 1998). The methodological underpinnings of the MBI have been criticized because it was developed inductively rather than on theoretical or clini cal foundations and (initially) assumed that burnout only happened to workers in people-facing jobs (Bianch et al., 2015). Other researchers have also questioned the cultural validity of some items (Kristensen et al., 2005). Therefore, while the MBI is undoubtedly the dominant burnout questionnaire, critics posit that “burnout is what the MBI measures and the MBI measures what burnou is” (p.193, Kristensen et al., 2005). Collectively, these issues raise some legitimate concerns about the validity of the MBI, particularly as a measure of non-organizationa burnout. This has led to the development of alternative measures to assess other forms of burnout, such as paren tal burnout (Roskam et al., 2018) and athlete burnou (Raedeke & Smith, 2001).

A validated measure that captures non-organizationa and job-related burnout is the Copenhagen Burnou Inventory (CBI; Kristensen et al., 2005); it uses three scales to assess ‘generic’ personal burnout, work-related burnout, and client-related burnout. A recent study adapted the CBI to investigate whether personal and aca demic burnout predicted ‘dropping out’ among autistic and non-autistic university students during the Covid-19 pandemic (Cage & McManemy, 2022). The study was the first to use the CBI with an autistic population and the findings showed that, while the autistic students reported higher personal and academic burnout rates than the non-autistic students, burnout predicted drop ping out only among the non-autistic students. The results lend support to a unique form of ‘autistic burnout’.

Despite the prolific amount of burnout research spanning 50 years, burnout syndrome is not included in the current Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders (DSM-5; APA, 2013), although it is mentioned in the latest International Classification of Diseases (ICD 11; World Health Organization, 2022) it is accompanied by strict parameters. The lack of formal recognition in the DSM may be partly due to the ongoing debate about whether burnout and depression are distinct constructs. Factors such as similar etiology and core symptom overlap, including anhedonia, fatigue, and withdrawal, contribute to this uncertainty (Bianchi et al., 2014; Bianchi et al., 2015; Tavella & Parker, 2020). A meta-analysis examining 14 samples from occupational burnout studies in various countries concluded that burnout is not a separate condition but instead belongs on the spectrum of depressive symptoms because its core facet (exhaustion) correlated more highly with depression than with other burnout facets (Bianchi et al., 2021; Bianchi & Sowden, 2022). However, the MBI was used to measure burnout in all the included studies, which, due to the methodological concerns described previously, may weaken the validity of these findings.

Occupational burnout research has shown that burn out can be a precursor to depression, with increased risk if burnout is severe (Ahola et al., 2005). Though a bidirectional relationship has been suggested, “the path from burnout to depression appears to be stronger than the path from depression to burnout” (p.109, Ahola & Hakanen, 2007). Autistic adults have differentiated between depression and autistic burnout in all qualitative studies so far, indicating that the two can co-occur and that autistic burnout can contribute to and exacerbate depression (Arnold et al., 2023b;Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020). Further more, qualitative research suggests that when autistic burnout and depression occur concurrently, the consequences can be particularly devastating and may include suicidal ideation (Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020). It is thus important to investigate whether a reciprocal relationship similar to that reported by Ahola and Hakanen (2007) exists between autistic burnout and depression.

Clarifying the distinction between autistic burnoutand depression is vital otherwise misdiagnosis and无效治疗的发生。用于治疗疑似抑郁的药物可能产生不良副作用，某些疗法可能不适合认知资源耗尽的急性孤独谱系耗竭患者。在Arnold等人（2023b）最近的一项研究中，一位参与者分享道：“我被误诊为双相⋯⋯而且耗竭仍然被归因于抑郁，但我知道其中的区别”（第9页）。症状没有改善可能导致进一步误诊为‘难治性抑郁症’（Souery et al., 2006）。早期研究表明，孤独谱系耗竭的适当恢复策略包括完全休息、减少认知需求、社交退缩、赋权与控制、提高自我意识以及精力管理（Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021;Raymaker et al., 2020），尽管支持这些策略的实证数据尚缺乏。

Academic Autism Spectrum Partnership inResearch and Education (AASPIRE) 通过与孤独谱系群体协商，采用社区参与式研究方法，开始了对孤独谱系耗竭的测量工作，并开发了 AASPIRE 孤独谱系耗竭量表 (ABM)。ABM 评估了文献中确定的孤独谱系耗竭的各个方面，包括认知能力下降、情绪调节能力下降、敏感性增加、日常能力下降、回避或退缩行为增加以及疲惫感增加。AASPIRE 团队对 80 名孤独谱系成人样本进行了 ABM 的验证，尽管结果尚未发表（D.Raymaker，个人通讯，2021 年 2 月 5 日）。Arnold 等人 (2023a) 首次报告并比较了 ABM 与其新测量工具——孤独谱系耗竭严重程度条目 (ABSI) 的部分心理测量学属性，样本为有孤独谱系耗竭经历的孤独谱系成人（N = 141）。他们的研究结果显示，通过患者健康问卷‑9 (PHQ‑9) 测量的抑郁与 ABM 之间存在强相关（r = 0.67）；PHQ‑9 与 ABSI 之间呈中等相关（r = 0.48），这凸显了需要更好地理解孤独谱系耗竭与抑郁之间的异同。值得注意的是，根据 ABM 的测量结果，伪装并不是孤独谱系耗竭的显著预测因子，尽管先前的研究一致报告它是一个重要的风险因素（Higgins 等人，2021；Mantzalas 等人，2021；Mantzalas 等人，2022；Raymaker 等人，2020）。ABM 在区分过去 3 个月内报告有（n = 103）和没有（n = 33）经历孤独谱系耗竭的参与者时，其特异度也较差，AUC 为 0.661（Arnold 等人，2023）。

尽管早期迹象表明孤独谱系耗竭可能在孤独谱系群体中较为常见，但发生率尚未见报道。已验证的心理测量工具可帮助研究者评估孤独谱系耗竭的发生率，并检查其与抑郁及其他变量（例如述情障碍、孤独谱系特质）的关系。已验证的测量工具还能协助研究者及

临床医师筛查孤独谱系耗竭，并监测其随时间变化的影响。因此，当前研究测试了ABM和CBI（个人量表和工作量表）在孤独谱系成人中的效度和因子结构。研究目标为：

1. 全面检验ABM和CBI（个人量表和工作量表）在孤独谱系样本中的心理测量学特性。2. 评估并比较ABM和CBI作为孤独谱系耗竭筛查工具的有效性。3. 检验倦怠测量与已验证的精神压力（抑郁、焦虑和压力）、疲劳以及幸福感（生活满意度、社会融合和社会贡献）测量工具之间的关系。

## 方法

## 参与者

在最初的451份调查回复中，有213份被排除，原因包括未填写参与者知情同意书、在所有调查问卷中均为“无应答者”、未回答注意力检查题项，或疑似为重复提交的问卷（这些问卷在人口学问题和开放式问题上给出了完全相同的答案）。该调查篇幅较长，预计完全完成需要约45分钟。对时间和日期戳的检查发现部分问卷完成速度较快；因此，完成时间在10分钟及以内的问卷也被排除。研究参与者为238名孤独谱系成人（71%为女性）年龄介于18至75岁之间（平均年龄 = 37.89岁，标准差.38）。超过一半的参与者处于某种形式的就业状态（55%），并拥有学士及以上学位（64%）。大多数参与者（69%）报告曾至少经历过一次孤独谱系耗竭，近半数参与者（46%）曾经历过四次或更多次孤独谱系耗竭。多数参与者（76%）表示他们有一种或多种共存的身体和/或心理健康状况。参与者的社会人口学特征如表1所示。

## 程序

本研究已获得拉筹伯大学人类研究伦理委员会批准（编号：HEC21009），并通过社交媒体进行招募。本研究属于一个博士研究项目的一部分，该项目的核心是提出并测试一个关于孤独谱系耗竭的风险与保护因素的概念模型（Mantzalas等人，2021）。该项目已由四位孤独谱系成人（3名女性：1名男性）组成的咨询小组审核并认可，这些成员均拥有晚发性孤独谱系诊断及孤独谱系耗竭的亲身经验。在线问卷由拉筹伯大学托管的REDCap（版本12.5.5）承载。为尽量减少自

ineffective treatments could occur. Medication to treat suspected depression may have adverse side effects, and some therapies may be inappropriate for individuals experiencing acute autistic burnout whose cognitive resources are depleted. In a recent study by Arnold et al. (2023b), one participant shared, “I was misdiagnosed with bipolar…also burnout is still blamed on depression, but I know the difference” (p.9). Failure to show symp tom improvement may lead to a further misdiagnosis of ‘treatment-resistant depression’ (Souery et al., 2006). Early research suggests that appropriate recovery strate gies for autistic burnout include complete rest, reduced cognitive demands, social withdrawal, empowerment and control, improved self-awareness, and energy manage ment (Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020), although empirical data to support these are lacking.

The Academic Autism Spectrum Partnership in Research and Education (AASPIRE) began the task of measuring autistic burnout by developing the AASPIRE Autistic Burnout Measure (ABM) using a communitybased participatory research approach in consultation with autistic people. The ABM assesses facets of autistic burnout identified in the literature including decreased cognitive abilities, decreased emotional regulation, increased sensitivity, decreased everyday abilities, increased avoidance or withdrawal, and increased exhaustion. The AASPIRE team validated the ABM with a sample of 80 autistic adults, although the results remain unpublished (D. Raymaker, personal communication, February 5, 2021). Arnold et al. (2023a) were the first to report and compare some psychometric properties of the ABM with their new measure, the Autistic Burnout Severity Items (ABSI), using a sample of autistic adults with experience of autistic burnout (N = 141). Their findings showed a strong correlation between depression measured by the Patient Health Questionnaire-9 (PHQ-9) and the ABM (r = 0.67); and a moderate correlation between the PHQ-9 and the ABSI (r = 0.48), highlighting the need to better understand similarities and differences among autistic burnout and depression. Of note, masking was not a significant predictor of autistic burnout as mea sured by the ABM, despite consistent reports that it is a strong risk factor (Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2022; Raymaker et al., 2020). The ABM also showed poor specificity with an AUC of 0.661 for differentiating between participants who reported they had (n = 103) and had not (n = 33) experienced autistic burnout during the previous 3 months (Arnold et al., 2023).

Despite the early indications that autistic burnou may be common among autistic people, prevalence rates have not been reported. Validated psychometric mea sures can assist researchers in gauging the prevalence of autistic burnout and examine its relationship with depres sion and other variables (e.g., alexithymia, autistic traits). Validated measures can also assist researchers and clinicians in screening for autistic burnout and monitoring its impact over time. Thus, the current study tests the validity and factor structure of both the ABM and CBI (personal and work scales) with autistic adults. The study aims were to:

1. Comprehensively examine the psychometric properties of the ABM and CBI (personal and work scales) with an autistic sample.

2. Evaluate and compare the efficacy of the ABM and CBI as screening tools for autistic burnout.

3. Examine the relationship between measures of burnout and validated measures of mental strain (depression, anxiety, and stress), fatigue, and wellbeing (satisfaction with life, social integration, and social contribution).

## METHOD

## Participants

Of the initial 451 survey responses, 213 were excluded for not completing the participant consent form, being ‘nonresponders’ on all survey questionnaires, not answering the attention check items, or were suspected duplicates that contained identical answers for the demographic and openended questions. The survey was lengthy and was estimated to take approximately 45 min to complete fully. Examination of the time and date stamps identified some surveys that were completed quickly; therefore, studies completed in 10 min or less were also excluded. The study participants were 238 autistic adults (71% female) aged between 18 and 75 years (M = 37.89, SD = 11.38). More than half of the participants were engaged in a form of employment (55%) and had earned a Bachelor’s degree or higher (64%). Most participants (69%) reported having experienced autistic burnout at least once, and nearly half had experienced autistic burnout four or more times (46%). Most participants indicated they had one or more co-occurring physical and/or mental health conditions (76%). The socio-demographic characteristics of participants are presented in Table 1.

## Procedure

The study was approved by the Human Research Ethics Committee at La Trobe University (reference HEC21009), and recruitment was conducted via social media. This study is part of a PhD research project that proposes and tests a conceptual model of risk and protective factors for autistic burnout (Mantzalas et al., 2021). The project was reviewed and endorsed by an Advisory Group of four autistic adults (3 female:1 male) with a late autism diagnosis and lived experience of autistic burnout. The online survey was hosted by REDCap (Version 12.5.5) hosted at La Trobe University. To minimize self 选择偏倚方面，本研究被宣传为一项调查可能影响孤独 谱系成人心理健康与福祉因素的研究，但提供了基于初 步定义的孤独谱系耗竭简要描述（Higgins et al., 2021；Raymaker et al., 2020）：“孤独谱系耗竭描 述的是长期累积的疲惫、感官敏感度增加以及技能丧失” 符合条件的参与者需年满18岁、具备英语语言能力，并 拥有正式的孤独谱系诊断或自我认同为孤独谱系。未要 求提供基于DSM的孤独谱系障碍诊断证明（APA, 2013）。采用28项孤独谱系商数（AQ‑Short； Hoekstra et al., 2011）作为筛查工具，所有参与者的 得分均超过建议的AQ‑Short截断值 >65，表明存在升 高的孤独谱系特质。完成调查后，参与者可选择参与抽 奖，赢得五张礼品券之一，价值50澳元或35美元。

## 测量

## 人口统计学

参与者完成了多项社会人口学问题，涉及年龄与性别、教育程度、就业状况、居住国家、诊断状态以及诊断或自我认同的年龄。参与者被问及是否经历过孤独谱系耗竭（以及经历次数），以及目前是否正在经历孤独谱系耗竭。参与者还被问及是否有任何共病的急性或慢性身体及心理健康状况，若有，请列出具体类型。参与者无需说明其共病状况是否经正式诊断，但部分个体在书面回复中自愿包含了这一信息。

selection bias, the study was advertised as an investiga tion of factors that may affect the mental health and well being of autistic adults, but a brief description of autistic burnout based on preliminary definitions (Higgins et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020) was provided: “Autis tic burnout describes long-term exhaustion, increased sensory sensitivities and loss of skills that builds up over time”. Eligible participants were 18 years of age or over, had English language competency, and had a forma Autism diagnosis or self-identified as autistic. Proof of a DSM-based diagnosis of Autism Spectrum Disorder (APA, 2013) was not requested. The 28-item Autism Spectrum Quotient (AQ-Short; Hoekstra et al., 2011) was used as a screening tool, with all participants’ scores exceeding the suggested AQ-Short cut-off of >65, indicat ing elevated autistic traits. Upon completing the survey, participants could opt into a prize draw to win one of five gift vouchers valued at \$50 AUD or \$35 USD.

## Measures

## Demographics

Participants completed various socio-demographic questions about their age and gender, level of education achieved, employment status, country of residence, diagnostic status, and age at diagnosis or selfidentification. Participants were asked whether, and how many times, they had experienced autistic burnout, and if they were currently experiencing autistic burnout. Participants were asked if they had any cooccurring acute or chronic physical and mental health conditions, and if so, to list which type(s). The participants were not asked to specify whether their cooccurring conditions were formally diagnosed but some individuals voluntarily included this information in the written responses.

## 孤独谱系耗竭

27条目AASPIRE孤独谱系耗竭量表（ABM）是一种新型的孤独谱系耗竭测量工具，要求参与者使用0（非常不同意）到4（非常同意）的五点李克特量表，对过去三个月内经历的各种症状相对于其自身典型水平进行评分。ABM评估孤独谱系耗竭的多个方面：认知能力下降（例如，“我解决难题的能力比平时更困难”）；情绪调节能力下降（例如，“我比平时更容易烦躁”）；敏感性增加（例如，“我经历的崩溃次数或强度比平时更多或更严重”）；能力下降（例如，“我管理工作或学校的难度比平时更大”）；回避/退缩增加（例如，“我比平时更频繁地回避需要努力的活动，即使我喜欢它们”）；以及疲惫感增加（例如，“我比平时感到更身体疲惫”）。参与者可在问卷末尾说明其作答原因。总分范围为0至108分，通过累加所有条目计算，分数越高表明经历孤独谱系耗竭的风险越大。ABM的使用已获得主要作者（D. Raymaker，个人通讯，2021年2月5日）的许可。本研究中ABM的麦克唐纳Ω系数（ω）为0.95。

## 倦怠

哥本哈根倦怠量表（CBI；Kristensen et al., 2005）从三个领域测量倦怠：个人倦怠（6个条目，例如“你多久感到精疲力竭？”）、工作相关倦怠（7个条目，例如“你的工作是否让你情绪耗竭？”）和客户相关倦怠（6个条目，例如“你是否觉得与客户合作很困难？”本研究仅使用了个人和工作相关子量表，参与者被告知工作’可指有偿或无偿就业、学习或照顾责任。所有条目采用5点李克特量表，个人子量表从1（总是）到5（从不/几乎从不），工作相关子量表从1（非常高程度）到5（非常低程度）。得分转换如下：总是/非常高程度= 100；经常/高程度 = 75；有时/中等程度 = 50；很少/低程度 = 25；从不/几乎从不/非常低程度 = 0。每个子量表的总分为所答条目得分的平均值，分数越高表示个人或工作相关倦怠程度越高。CBI个人（CBI‑P）和工作（CBI‑W）子量表先前已在孤独谱系样本中使用（Cronbach's α分别为0.86和0.89；Cage &McManemy, 2022）。在本研究中， ω 对CBI‑P为0.80，对CBI‑W为0.87。

## 抑郁

病人健康问卷（PHQ‑9；Kroenke 等, 2001）是一项包含9个条目的自评抑郁症状测量工具，用于评估过去两周的症状（例如“情绪低落、抑郁或无望”），并已验证可用于孤独谱系成人（Arnold 等, 2020）。九个条目采用4点李克特量表评分，从0（完全没有）到3（几乎每天），求和得到总分0至27分，其中0‑4分表示无抑郁或极少抑郁；5‑9分轻度抑郁；10‑14分中度抑郁；15‑19分中重度抑郁；20‑27分重度抑郁。本研究中PHQ‑9的 ω为0.89。

## 焦虑

广泛性焦虑量表（GAD‑7；Spitzer 等, 2006）是一份包含7个条目的自评量表，用于测量过去两周内的焦虑症状（例如，“感到害怕，好像有什么可怕的事情会发生”）。所有条目采用4点李克特量表评分，从0（完全没有）到3（几乎每天）。将7个条目的得分相加，得到总焦虑严重程度评分，范围在0到21分之间，其中0‑4分表示最低严重程度；5‑9分为轻度；10‑14分为中度；15‑21分为重度。GAD‑7此前已被用于孤独谱系成人（α = 0.88；Griffiths 等, 2019）。本研究中GAD‑7的 为0.90。

## 压力

抑郁焦虑压力量表‑21（DASS‑21；Lovibond &Lovibond, 1995）是DASS‑42的简版，用于测量过去一周内与抑郁、焦虑和压力症状相关的自我报告痛苦。DASS‑21已验证可用于孤独谱系成人（α = 0.88；Park 等, 2020）。三个子量表各包含七个项目，但本研究仅使用压力子量表。子量表的项目相加后乘以二得出总分，其中0至14分表示正常压力水平；15至18分表示轻度压力；19至25分表示中度压力；26至33分表示重度压力；分数 >34表示极重度压力水平。在本研究中，压力子量表的 ω为0.88。

## 掩饰/伪装

孤独谱系掩饰特征问卷（CAT‑Q；Hull 等, 2018）是一份包含25个条目的自评问卷，用于测量孤独谱系群体和非孤独谱系群体使用的社交掩饰行为。该问卷评估了

## Autistic burnout

The 27-item AASPIRE Autistic Burnout Measure (ABM) is a new measure of autistic burnout that asks participants to rate a range of symptoms experienced over the past three months compared to what they con sider typical for them using a 5-point Likert scale from 0 (strongly disagree) to 4 (strongly agree). The ABM assesses various facets of autistic burnout: decreased cog nitive abilities (e.g., “I’ve had a harder time solving chal lenging problems than I usually do”); decreased emotional regulation (e.g., “I’ve been feeling more irrita ble than I usually do”); increased sensitivity (e.g., “I’ve had more, or more severe, meltdowns than I usually do”); decreased abilities (e.g., “I’ve had a harder time managing work or school than I usually do”); increased avoidance/withdrawal (e.g., I’ve been avoiding activities that require effort, even if I like them, more often than I usually do); and increased exhaustion (e.g., “I’ve fel more physically exhausted than I usually do”). Partici pants can provide reasons for their responses at the end of the questionnaire. A total score between 0 and 108 is calculated by summing all items, with higher scores indi cating a greater risk of experiencing autistic burnout. The ABM was used with permission from the lead author (D. Raymaker, personal communication, February 5, 2021). McDonald’s omega coefficient (ω) for the ABM in the current study was 0.95.

## Burnout

The Copenhagen Burnout Inventory (CBI; Kristensen et al., 2005) measures burnout across three domains: per sonal burnout (6 items, e.g., “How often do you feel worn out?”), work-related burnout (7 items, e.g., “Is your work emotionally exhausting?”), and client-related burnou (6 items, e.g., “Do you find it hard to work with cli ents?”). Only the personal and work-related subscales were used in this study, and participants were advised that ‘work’ could refer to paid or unpaid employment, study, or caring responsibilities. All items use a 5-point Likert scale from 1 (Always) to 5 (Never/almost never) for the personal subscale and from 1 (To a very high degree) to 5 (To a very low degree) for the work-related subscale. Scores are converted as follows: Always/To a very high degree = 100; Often/To a high degree = 75; Sometimes/Somewhat = 50; Seldom/To a low degree = 25; and Never/almost never /To a very low degree = 0. The total for each subscale is calculated as the average of the items answered, where higher scores indicate higher levels of personal or work-related burn out. The CBI personal (CBI-P) and work (CBI-W) sub scales have previously been used with an autistic sample (Cronbach’s 0.86 and 0.89, respectively; Cage & McManemy, 2022). In the current study, ω for the CBI-P was 0.80 and 0.87 for the CBI-W 0.87.

## Depression

The Patient Health Questionnaire (PHQ-9; Kroenke et al., 2001) is a 9-item measure of self-reported depressive symptoms during the previous two weeks (e.g., “Feeling down, depressed, or hopeless”) and has been validated for use with autistic adults (Arnold et al., 2020). The nine items are scored on a 4-point Likert scale from 0 (not at all) to 3 (nearly every day) and summed to achieve a total score between 0 and 27, where scores between 0 and 4 indicate minimal depression; between 5 and 9 mild depression; between 10 and 14 moderate depression; between 15 and 19 moderately severe depression, and between 20 and 27 indicate severe depression. The ω for the PHQ-9 in the current study was 0.89.

## Anxiety

The General Anxiety Disorder Scale (GAD-7; Spitze et al., 2006) is a 7-item measure of self-reported anxiety symptoms during the previous two weeks (e.g., “Feeling afraid as if something awful might happen”). All items are scored on a 4-point Likert scale from 0 (not at all) to 3 (nearly every day). The 7-item responses are summed to achieve a total anxiety severity score between 0 and 21, where scores between 0 and 4 indicate minimal severity; between 5 and 9 mild; between 10 and 14 moderate; and between 15 and 21 severe. The GAD-7 has previously been used with autistic adults (α = 0.88; Griffiths et al., 2019). The ω for the GAD-7 in the present study was 0.90.

## Stress

The Depression Anxiety Stress Scale-21 (DASS-21; Lovibond & Lovibond, 1995) is an abbreviated version of the DASS-42 that measures self-reported distress associated with depression, anxiety, and stress symptoms over the previous week. The DASS-21 has been validated for use with autistic adults (α = 0.88; Park et al., 2020). Each of the three subscales contains seven items, but only the Stress sub scale was used in this study. Subscale items are added together and multiplied by two to achieve a total score where values between 0 and 14 indicate normal levels of stress; between 15 and 18 mild stress; between 19 and 25 moderate stress; between 26 and 33 severe stress; and scores >34 indicate extremely severe levels of stress. The ω for the stress subscale was 0.88 in the current study.

## Camouflaging/masking

The Camouflaging Autism Traits Questionnaire (CAT-Q; Hull et al., 2018), is a 25-item self-report questionnairethat measures social camouflaging behaviors used byautistic and non-autistic people. The CAT-Q assesses three掩饰行为的三个因素：补偿（9个条目；“当我与人互动时，我会刻意模仿对方的肢体语言或面部表情”）；伪装（8个条目：“我会监控自己的肢体语言或面部表情，以便让自己看起来放松”）；以及同化（8个条目；“在社交场合中，我感觉自己‘在表演’而非做自己”）所有条目均采用李克特量表评分，其中1 =非常不同意，2 =不同意， 3 = 有些不同意， 4 = 既不同意也不反对， 5 = 有些同意， 6 =同意， 7 =非常同意。五个条目为反向计分。伪装和同化因素的总分范围在8至56分之间，补偿因素的总分范围在9至63分之间，分数越高表示掩饰策略使用越多。此前已证实总量表（Cronbach's alpha 0.94）及各因素均具有较高的内部一致性，其中补偿因素的Cronbach's alpha为0.91，伪装为0.85，同化为0.92（Hull 等, 2018）。在当前研究中，CAT‑Q（总量表）的 ω 为0.91。

## 疲劳

弗林德斯疲劳量表（FFS；Gradisar 等，2007）是一个包含7个条目的量表，用于测量过去两周内经历的日间疲劳特征（例如频率、严重程度、后果）。其中六个条目采用李克特量表，评分从0（完全没有）到4（极度）还有一个条目测量受访者感到疲劳的时间段（例如清晨、中午、下午晚些时候）。各条目得分相加得到总分，范围从0到31，其中13至15分表示临界疲劳；16至20分为中度疲劳；而≥21 分表示高度疲劳（Cameron 等，2017）。FFS此前已用于孤独谱系成人（α = 0.84；Baker & Richdale，2015）。本研究中FFS的 ω 为0.86。

## 生活满意度

生活满意度量表（SWLS；Diener 等，1985）是一个包含5个条目的整体生活满意度测量工具。条目采用李克特量表评分，从1（非常不同意）到7（非常同意）。各条目得分相加得到总分，范围从5到35，分数越高表示生活满意度越高。SWLS此前已用于孤独谱系成人（α = 0.89；Casagrande 等，2020）。本研究中SWLS的 ω为0.89。

## 幸福感

社会幸福感量表（Keyes, 1998）用于测量个体对其状况的评价以及

在五个领域的社会功能：社会整合（SI）；社会接纳；社会贡献（SC）；社会实现；以及社会一致性。每个子量表使用7点李克特量表，从1（非常不同意）到7（非常同意）。本研究仅使用了SI和SC子量表。SI子量表包含7个项目（例如，“你感觉自己是社区的重要一员”），其中第1和第6项是反向计分。SC子量表包含6个项目（例如，你认为自己有有价值的东西可以贡献给世界”），其中第3、4和6项是反向计分。子量表分数通过将各个项目的得分相加，再除以子量表中的项目数来计算。在本研究中，SI和SC子量表的 ω分别为0.91和0.80。

## 统计分析

大部分数据分析使用社会科学统计软件包（SPSS，版本28）进行。半探索性因子模型使用AMOS（版本29）构建，信度值使用R psych::omega包（版本2023.06.1）计算。数据清理后，检测到202个缺失值（占总数据集的0.33%，每份问卷最多1.12%）。由于缺失数据比例小于5%且为随机缺失，使用期望最大化法在SPSS中替换缺失数据点（Tabachnick & Fidell, 2013）。所有问卷的描述性统计见表2。偏度和峰度值均在可接受范围内 +3和 3（Tabachnick & Fidell, 2013）。

分析分两部分进行：第一部分针对本研究的第一和第二个目标（评估耗竭测量工具的心理测量学属性及其作为孤独谱系耗竭筛查工具的有效性），第二部分针对第三个目标（考察孤独谱系耗竭与其他变量之间的关系）。

采用相关分析来探讨变量之间的关系，并评估耗竭测量工具的收敛效度、区分效度和判别效度。对相关性≥0.30进行了评估。由于所有测量工具均使用李克特量表，因此采用斯皮尔曼相关（r）进行解释。根据自我报告的孤独谱系耗竭状态（是；否；不确定），采用Welch's F统计量检验组间差异，因为各组样本量不相等。效应量以Omega平方（ω<sup>2</sup>）表示，其中0.01、0.06和0.14分别表示小、中、大效应量（Kirk, 1996）。在适当情况下采用Games‑Howell事后检验分析，其中Cohen's d值0.20、0.50和0.80分别表示小、中、大效应量（Kirk, 1996）。

探索性因子分析（EFA）采用主成分（PC）因子分析，使用正交（Varimax）旋转。

factors of camouflaging behavior: Compensation (9 items; “When I am interacting with someone, I deliberately copy their body language or facial expressions”); Masking (8 items; “I monitor my body language or facial expressions so that I appear relaxed”); and Assimilation (8 items; “In social situations, I feel like I’m ‘performing’ rather than being myself”). All items are rated on a Likert scale where 1 = Strongly disagree, 2 = Disagree, 3 = Somewhat dis agree, 4 = Neither agree nor disagree, 5 = Somewha agree, 6 = Agree, and 7 = Strongly agree. Five items are reverse scored. The total scores for the Masking and Assim ilation factors can range between 8 and 56, and between 9 and 63 for the Compensation factor, where higher scores indicate greater use of camouflaging strategies. High inter nal consistency has previously been demonstrated both for the total scale (Cronbach’s alpha 0.94) and individual fac tors, with Cronbach’s alphas of 0.91 for Compensation, 0.85 for Masking, and 0.92 for Assimilation (Hul et al., 2018). The ω for the CAT-Q (total) was 0.91 in the current study.

## Fatigue

The Flinders Fatigue Scale (FFS; Gradisar et al., 2007) is a 7-item scale that measures characteristics of daytime fatigue experienced over the previous two weeks (e.g., frequency, severity, consequences). Six items use a Likert scale ranging from 0 (not at all) to 4 (extremely), and one item measures the time-of-day respondents expe rienced fatigue (e.g., early morning, midday, late after noon). Items are summed to achieve a total score ranging from 0 to 31, where scores between 13 and 15 indicate borderline fatigue; 16–20 moderate fatigue; and scores ≥21 indicate high levels of fatigue (Cameron et al., 2017). The FFS has been previously used with autistic adults (α = 0.84; Baker & Richdale, 2015). The ω for the FFS was 0.86 in this study.

## Life satisfaction

The Satisfaction with Life Scale (SWLS; Diener et al., 1985) is a 5-item measure of overall life satisfaction. Items are rated on a Likert scale from 1 (Strongly disagree) to 7 (Strongly agree). Responses are summed to achieve a total score between 5 and 35, where higher scores indicate greater satisfaction with life. The SWLS has been used previously with autistic adults (α = 0.89; Casagrande et al., 2020). The ω for the SWLS in this study was 0.89.

## Wellbeing

The Social Well-Being Scale (Keyes, 1998) measures indi viduals’ assessment of their circumstances and functioning in society across five domains: social integration (SI); social acceptance; social contribution (SC); social actualization; and social coherence. Each subscale uses a 7-point Likert scale from 1 (Strongly disagree) to 7 (Strongly agree). Only the SI and SC subscales were used in this study. The SI subscale consists of 7 items (e.g., “You feel like you’re an important part of your community”), where items 1 and 6 are reverse scored. The SC subscale consists of 6 items (e.g., You think you have something valuable to give to the world”) where items 3, 4, and 6 are reverse scored. Subscale scores are calculated by adding the individual item scores and dividing them by the number of items in the subscale. In the current study, ω for the SI and SC subscales was 0.91 and 0.80, respectively.

## Statistical analyses

Most data analyses were conducted with Statistical Pack age for the Social Sciences (SPSS, Version 28). Semiexploratory factor models were constructed using AMOS (Version 29) and reliability values calculated using the R psych::omega package (Version 2023.06.1). After cleaning the data, 202 missing values were detected (0.33% of the total dataset and a maximum of 1.12% per individual questionnaire). Given the proportion of missing data was less than 5% and the values were missing at random, the Expectation Maximization method was used to substitute missing data points in SPSS (Tabachnick & Fidell, 2013). Descriptive statistics for all questionnaires are provided in Table 2. Skewness and kurtosis values fell within the acceptable range of +3 and 3 (Tabachnick & Fidell, 2013).

Analyses were conducted in two parts: part one addressed the first and second aims of this study (evaluating the psychometric properties of the burnout measures, and their efficacy as screening tools for autistic burnout), and part two addressed the third aim (examining the relationship between autistic burnout and other variables).

Correlations were used to explore relationships among variables, and assess convergent, divergent, and discriminant validity of the burnout measures. Correlations ≥0.30 were evaluated. Spearman correlations (r ) were interpreted because all measures used Likert scales. Group differences according to self-reported autistic burnout status (Yes; No; Unsure) were examined using Welch’s F statistic as the group sizes were unequal, with effect sizes represented by Omega squared (ω<sup>2</sup>) where 0.01, 0.06, and 0.14 represented small, medium, and large effect sizes, respectively (Kirk, 1996). Games-Howell post-hoc analyses were used where appropriate, where Cohen’s d values of 0.20, 0.50, and 0.80 represented small, medium and large effect sizes, respectively (Kirk, 1996).

Exploratory factor analysis (EFA) used principal components (PC) factoring with orthogonal (Varimax)

注：N = 238。缩略语：ABM，AASPIRE孤独谱系耗竭量表；CAT‑Q，掩饰孤独谱系特质问卷；CBI‑P，哥本哈根倦怠量表—个人子量表；CBI‑W，哥本哈根倦怠量表—工作子量表；DASS‑S，抑郁焦虑压力量表‑21（压力子量表）；FFS，弗林德斯疲劳量表；GAD‑7，广泛性焦虑障碍量表‑7；PHQ‑9，患者健康问卷‑9；SC，社会贡献子量表；SI，社会融合子量表；SWLS，生活满意度量表。

因子保留的决策参考了基于1000个随机相关矩阵的平行分析，比较平均特征值（Vivek et al., 2017），以及Velicer的最小平均偏相关（MAP）检验（OConnor, 2000）。保留决策还参考了方差解释百分比、共同度数值以及因子是否具有理论意义。保留条目的最小载荷为0.30。为了检验区分效度，还对ABM、CBI‑P、CBI‑W、PHQ‑9和FFS条目进行了EFA（PC；Promax旋转）。校正项目‑总分相关用于评估因子信度其中最小相关值为0.30可接受，较高的值表示因子信度更高（Zijlmans et al., 2018）。

采用半探索性模型检验ABM和CBI‑P（初步分析后排除CBI‑W）的因子效度。这些模型包含探索性因子分析识别的因素，但问卷条目不加约束，可自由加载或交叉加载到任何因子。为每种倦怠测量拟合了四个探索性模型：(1)单一般因子模型；(2)相关因子模型；(3)单层次模型；(4)单双因子模型。模型拟合评估采用卡方（χ<sup>2</sup>）、近似均方根误差（RMSEA） ≥0。06 =良好拟合；≥0。08 = 可接受拟合；比较拟合指数（CFI）>0。95 = 良好拟合；Tucker‑Lewis指数（TLI） >0。90 =可接受拟合；标准化均方根残差（SRMR）≥0。06 = 良好拟合； ≥0。08 = 可接受拟合（Reise et al., 2013）。为确定最简约模型，还比较了赤池信息准则（AIC）、贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）和一致性赤池信息准则（CAIC）值，其中最小值代表最佳拟合模型（Tabachnick & Fidell, 2013）。我们尝试拟合

包含倦怠、抑郁、焦虑、压力和疲劳测量所有条目的探索性模型，但由于参数过多无法生成可识别的模型。

通过检验ABM和CBI‑P的维度与信度，确定项目方差中有多少可归因于一个总括性的“一般”因子，以及“特定”因子（Reise, 2012; Rodriguez et al.,2016a）。由于ABM的因子结构尚未被报告，且仅有一项其他研究在孤独谱系人群中使用了CBI（Cage &McManemy, 2022），因此采用适用于探索性因子模型（Flora, 2020）的R psych::omega包计算信度估计。该方法基于高阶探索性因子结构生成分层omega（ω ）值，其中“每个项目在每个低阶因子上都有非零因子载荷”（Flora, 2020, Data S1, p. 7）。由于高阶因子通过低阶因子间接影响量表项目，探索性 ω 在概念上与验证性因子分析模型中的omega高阶（ω ）相似（Flora, 2020）。对分层omega（ω ）以及omega一般、组和总值进行解读，以获得潜在总分和子量表分数的信度估计。一般而言，较高的 ω 分数（>0.80）表明量表具有单维度性，而子量表信度值较低则表明大多数方差可归因于一般因子（Rodriguez et al., 2016b）。采用Schmid‑Leiman因子变换进一步考察高阶因子与低阶因子及量表项目之间的关系（Wolff & Preising,2005）。

耗竭测量工具的评分者间信度通过使用最大似然估计法的组内相关系数(ICC)分析进行评估。设计为双向、一致性、单一评分、随机效应

Abbreviations: ABM, AASPIRE Autistic Burnout Measure; CAT-Q, Camouflaging Autistic Traits Questionnaire; CBI-P, Copenhagen Burnout Inventory—Personal subscale; CBI-W, Copenhagen Burnout Inventory—Work subscale; DASS-S, Depression Anxiety Stress Scale-21 (stress subscale); FFS, Flinders Fatigue Scale; GAD-7, Generalized Anxiety Disorder scale-7; PHQ-9, Patient Health Questionnaire-9; SC, Social Contribution subscale; SI, Social Integration subscale; SWLS, Satisfaction With Life Scale.

rotation. Factor retention was informed by parallel anal ysis using 1000 random correlation matrices and compar ing mean eigenvalues (Vivek et al., 2017), and Velicer’s minimum average partial (MAP) tests (O’Connor, 2000). Retention decisions were additionally informed by the percentage of variance explained, communalities values, and whether the factor(s) made theoretical sense. Retained items had a minimum loading of 0.30. To examine divergent validity, EFA (PC; Promax rotation) was also conducted using the ABM, CBI-P, CBI-W, PHQ-9, and FFS items. Corrected item-total correlations were used to assess factor reliability, where minimum cor relations of 0.30 were acceptable and higher values indi cated greater factor reliability (Zijlmans et al., 2018).

Semi-exploratory models were used to examine the factorial validity of the ABM and CBI-P (the CBI-W was excluded after initial analyses). The models included fac tors identified from EFA, but the questionnaire items were unconstrained and free to load or cross-load onto any factor(s). Four exploratory models were fitted for each burnout measure: (1) a single general factor model; (2) a correlated factors model; (3) a single hierarchica model; and (4) a single bifactor model. Model fit was evaluated using chi-square (χ<sup>2</sup>), the root mean square error of approximation (RMSEA) ≥0.06 = good fit; ≥0.08 = fair fit; comparative fit index (CFI) >0.95 = good fit; Tucker Lewis index (TLI) >0.90 = fair fit; and standardized root mean residual (SRMR) ≥0.06 = good fit; ≥0.08 = fair fit (Reise et al., 2013). To identify the most parsimonious model, Akaike Informa tion Criterion (AIC), Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) and Consistent Akaike Information Criterion (CAIC) values were also compared, where the smallest value represents the best fitting model (Tabachnick & Fidell, 2013). We attempted to fit exploratory models with all items from the burnout, depression, anxiety, stress, and fatigue measures, however there were too many parameters to produce identified models.

Dimensionality and reliability of the ABM and CBI-P were examined to determine the amount of item variance that could be attributed to an overarching ‘general’ factor, and to ‘specific’ factors (Reise, 2012; Rodriguez et al., 2016a). As the factor structure of the ABM has not been reported, and one other study has used the CBI with an autistic population (Cage & McManemy, 2022), reliability estimates were calculated with the R psych::omega package which is appropriate for exploratory factor models (Flora, 2020). This method produces omega hierarchical (ω ) values based on a higher-order exploratory factor structure where “every item has a non-zero factor loading on each lower-order factor” (Flora, 2020, Data S1, p. 7). As the higher-order factor indirectly affects scale items through the lowerorder factors, exploratory ω is conceptually similar to omega-higher order (ω ) in a CFA model (Flora, 2020). Omega hierarchical (ω ) and the omega general, group, and total values were interpreted to obtain the reliability estimates for the latent total and subscale scores. In gen eral, higher ω<sub>h</sub> scores (>0.80) suggest scale unidimensionality and low subscale reliability values indicate that most variance is attributable to the general factor (Rodriguez et al., 2016b). The Schmid-Leiman factor transformation was applied to further examine the relationships between the higher and lower-order factors and scale items (Wolff & Preising, 2005).

Interrater reliability of the burnout measures was assessed with intraclass correlations (ICC) analyses using the maximum likelihood estimation method. The design was a two-way, consistency, single rating, random effects 模型（ten Hove et al.，2022）。信度值低于0.5被视 为较差；介于0.51至0.75之间为中等；介于0.76至0.9 之间为良好；高于0.9为优秀（Koo & Li，2016）。

受试者工作特征（ROC）分析通过比较ABM、CBI‑P和CBI‑W在检测当前是否经历孤独谱系耗竭的参与者（n = 76）与未经历者（n = 87）中的表现，评估了这些工具作为孤独谱系耗竭筛查工具的有效性。在本分析中，“否”和“不确定”组合并为一个类别“否/不确定”（n = 87）。计算了曲线下面积（AUC），它代表随机选取的一名正在经历孤独谱系耗竭的个体在倦怠测量上的得分高于随机选取的一名未经历孤独谱系耗竭的个体的概率。数值越高表示判别效度越好。通过约登指数确定每项测量的最佳截断值，并利用交叉表计算灵敏度、特异度、阳性预测值、阴性预测值、阳性似然比、阴性似然比、发生率和总体检验准确度。鉴于样本中抑郁、焦虑和压力的高共现性，以及关于耗竭‑抑郁重叠的争议，本研究还针对PHQ‑9、GAD‑7、DASS‑S和FFS进行了ROC分析和交叉表，以检验它们在当前样本中检测孤独谱系耗竭的有效性。

检查了ABM和CBI‑P与抑郁、焦虑、压力和疲劳之间的相关大小，以评估耗竭测量工具与PHQ‑9、DASS‑S、GAD‑7和FFS之间的区分效度。我们使用了基于Meng et al.（1992；IBM SPSS支持）的语法，并按以下顺序输入值：r23；r12；r13 n。收敛效度和区分效度使用r12–r13计算。

## 结果

## 描述性统计

研究问卷的描述性统计见表2。平均而言，参与者在焦虑（GAD‑7）、中度抑郁（PHQ‑9）、中度压力以及中度至高疲劳水平（FFS）方面均超过了临床截断值，且对生活略有不满。在自报目前正在经历孤独谱系耗竭的参与者（n = 76）中，大多数人的PHQ‑9抑郁得分达到或超过截断值（88.16%），相比之下，“否”组（n= 59）为50.85%，“不确定”组（n = 28）为78.57%。对于“认为死了更好或想以某种方式伤害自己”这一问题，63.16%的“是”组参与者表示在过去

两周内，他们有过这种想法的天数占部分天数、大部分天数或几乎每天，相比之下，“否”组为22%，“不确定”组为35.71%。

## 评估ABM和CBI的心理测量学属性

## 构念效度

自我报告他们目前正在经历孤独谱系耗竭（'是'；n = 76）、没有经历（'否'；n = 59）或不确定（'不确定'；n = 28）的参与者的ABM、CBI‑P和CBI‑W得分存在显著差异。事后分析显示，'是'和'否'组、'是'和'不确定'组、以及'否和'不确定'组的参与者ABM平均得分存在统计显著的差异。相比之下，CBI‑P和CBI‑W仅发现'是'和'否'组之间存在统计显著的均值差异。每个倦怠状态组的均值和标准差、Welch's F结果和Omega平方（ω<sup>2</sup>）效应量见表3。事后分析结果见表4。

## 因子结构

## 识别27条目AASPIRE孤独谱系耗竭量表的因子结构

KMO值（0.926）和巴特利特球形检验（χ<sup>2</sup>(351)= 4792.65, p < 0.001）均支持对ABM进行因子分析的适用性。抽样充分性测度（MSA）和共同度值表明所有27个条目均可保留用于EFA。特征值的初步检验显示四因子解，而平行分析则建议三因子解（表S1）。由于第四成分的均值仅略低于其对应的模拟值（M = 1.407vs M = 1.497），且纳入第四因子后方差解释百分比从59.89%提高至65.10%，因此最终保留了四个因子。四因子解在理论上也有意义，并得到了Velicer的MAP检验以及保留特征值 >1的Kaiser‑Guttman准则的支持。

第一个因子“认知与功能困难”包含九个项目（项目 1、2、3、4、5、14、19、20、21）。第二个因子“情绪与感觉失调”包含八个项目（项目 6、7、8、9、10、11、12、13）。第三个因子“回避与耗竭”包含六个项目（项目 22、23、24、25、26、27），第四个因子“社交与沟通困难”包含四个项目（项目 15、16、17、18）。表 5 显示了因子载荷、 系数以及四个ABM 因子的方差解释百分比。

大多数校正后的项目间相关落在0.30–0.70的可接受范围内（Ferketich, 1991）。部分项目冗余由以下非常强的相关所提示：

model (ten Hove et al., 2022). Reliability values below 0.5 were considered poor; between 0.51 and 0.75 moder ate; between 0.76 and 0.9 good; and above 0.9, excellen (Koo & Li, 2016).

Receiver operator characteristic (ROC) analyses assessed the effectiveness of the ABM, CBI-P and CBI-W as screening tools for autistic burnout by comparing how well they were able to detect autistic burnout among par ticipants who indicated they were (n = 76) or were no currently experiencing autistic burnout. For this analysis, the ‘No’ and ‘Unsure’ groups were merged into a single category ‘No/Unsure’ (n = 87). Area under the curve (AUC) was computed, which represents the chance that a randomly selected individual who is experiencing autistic burnout will score higher on a burnout measure than a randomly selected individual who is not experiencing autistic burnout. Higher values represent greater discriminant validity. Youden’s index determined optimal cut off scores for each measure, and crosstabulations were used to calculate sensitivity, specificity, positive and neg ative predictive values, positive and negative likelihood ratios, prevalence and overall test accuracy. Given the high co-occurrence of depression, anxiety, and stress in the sample, and debate regarding a burnout-depression overlap, ROC analyses and crosstabulations were also conducted with the PHQ-9, GAD-7, DASS-S and FFS to examine their effectiveness for detecting autistic burnou in the current sample.

The magnitude of the correlations between the ABM and CBI-P with depression, anxiety, stress, and fatigue were examined to evaluate divergent validity between the burnout measures and PHQ-9, DASS-S, GAD-7, and FFS. We used syntax based on Meng et al., (1992; IBM SPSS Support) and values were entered in the following order: r23; r12; r13 n. Convergent and divergent validity were calculated using r12–r13.

## RESULTS

## Descriptive statistics

The descriptives for the study questionnaires are shown in Table 2. On average, participants exceeded the clinica cut-offs for anxiety (GAD-7), moderate depression (PHQ-9), moderate stress, and moderate to high levels of fatigue (FFS) and were slightly dissatisfied with life. The majority of participants who self-reported they were cur rently experiencing autistic burnout (n = 76) scored at or above the cut-off score for depression on the PHQ-9 (88.16%), compared to 50.85% in the ‘No’ group (n = 59), and 78.57% of participants in the ‘Unsure group (n = 28). In response to the question, “Thoughts that you would be better off dead or of hurting yourself in some way”, 63.16% of participants in the ‘Yes’ group indicated they had experienced such thoughts some of the day, most of the day or almost every day over the previous two weeks, compared to 22% of those in the ‘No’ group and 35.71% in the ‘Unsure’ group.

## Evaluating the psychometric properties of the ABM and CBI

Construct validity

The ABM, CBI-P and CBI-W scores for participants who self-reported that they were (‘Yes’; n = 76), were not (‘No’; n = 59) or were unsure (‘Unsure’; n = 28), if they were currently experiencing autistic burnout differed significantly. Post-hoc analyses showed statistically significant differences in the mean ABM scores of participants in the ‘Yes’ and ‘No’ groups; ‘Yes’ and ‘Unsure groups; and ‘No’ and ‘Unsure’ groups. In comparison, the only statistically significant mean differences found for the CBI-P and CBI-W were between the ‘Yes’ and ‘No’ groups. The means and standard deviations for each burnout status group, Welch’s F results and omega squared (ω<sup>2</sup>) effect sizes are shown in Table 3. Results of post-hoc analyses are shown in Table 4.

## Factor structure

Both the KMO (0.926) and Bartlett’s test of sphericity (χ<sup>2</sup> (351) = 4792.65, p < 0.001) endorsed the appropriateness of factor analysis for the ABM. Measures of sampling adequacy (MSA) and communalities values indicated all 27 items could be retained for EFA. Initial inspection of the eigenvalues indicated a 4-factor solution, whereas parallel analysis suggested a 3-factor solution (Table S1). As the mean value of the fourth component was only slightly lower than its corresponding simulated value (M = 1.407 compared to M = 1.497), and the percentage of variance explained increased from 59.89% to 65.10% upon inclusion of the fourth factor, four factors were ulti mately retained. The 4-factor solution also made theoreti cal sense and was endorsed by Velicer’s MAP test, and the Kaiser-Guttman rule of retaining eigenvalues >1.

The first factor, ‘Cognitive and Functioning Difficulty comprised nine items (items 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 14, 19, 20, 21). The second factor, ‘Emotional and Sensory Dysregulation’, consisted of eight items (items 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13). The third factor, ‘Avoidance and Exhaustion comprised six items (items 22, 23, 24, 25, 26, 27), and the fourth factor, ‘Social and Communication Difficulty’ consisted of four items (items 15, 16, 17, 18). Table 5 shows the factor loadings, ω coefficients, and percentage of variance explained for the four ABM factors.

Most corrected inter-item correlations fell withinacceptable ranges of 0.30–0.70 (Ferketich, 1991). Someitem redundancy was suggested by very strong项目17（“我比平时更难向他人表达我的观点”）与项目18（“我比平时更难找到合适的词语来表达我的意思”0.807）之间，以及项目22（“我比平时更频繁地回避社交场合，即使我喜欢它们”）与项目23（“我比平时更想与他人隔离自己”；0.784）之间的相关。项目6（“我比平时更难控制冲动”）和项目11（“我比平时发生更多或更严重的崩溃”）与来自回避与耗竭因子（项目22、23、24、25）的所有回避相关项目之间存在虚弱相关。然而，所有ABM条目最终均予以保留，因为删除它们不会提高信度，并且所有条目与其各自因子之间的校正项目‑总分相关均较强（>0.60；见表S2）。四个ABM因子之间的因子间相关良好（>0.50），如表S3所示。

## 识别哥本哈根倦怠量表的个人与工作分量表的因子结构

2KMO（0.857）和巴特利特球形检验 $\begin{array} { r } { ( \chi ( 7 8 ) = 1 4 0 8 . } \end{array}$ 69, p < 0.001）均确认了因子分析适用于两个CBI量表。共同度值表明，所有6个CBI‑P条目和7个CBI‑W条目均可保留用于EFA。对特征值的检查提示了一个三因子解，该解得到平行分析的支持，并解释了64.57%的方差。CBI‑W的条目1‑6归入第一个因子“工作相关耗竭”，而CBI‑P的条目均匀地负载到两个因子上：因子二“情绪耗竭”（条目3、5、4）和因子三“身体耗竭”（条目1、2、6）。CBI‑W的条目7（“你在闲暇时间是否有足够的精力陪伴家人和朋友？”）负载到情绪耗竭上，但

该条目被舍弃，因为将其排除后因子的信度从 ω = 0.775提升至 ω = 0.795。

CBI‑W和CBI‑P是独立的量表，可以分别施测和计分（Kristensen et al., 2005）。在我们包含两个CBI量表的初步因子分析中，保留下来的CBI‑W条目载荷于工作相关耗竭因子，该因子解释了总方差的41.06%。为了检验这是否低估了CBI‑P的结构，我们仅使用6个CBI‑P条目重复了EFA。平行分析和Velicer的MAP检验提示单因子解（表S1），但最终保留了双因子（情绪耗竭和身体耗竭），因为双因子解将方差解释率从52.45%提高到69.60%。表6a展示了三因子CBI解的因子载荷、ω 系数和方差解释百分比，表6b呈现了双因子CBI‑P解的值。三因子和双因子解的信度系数略低于ABM总分及各因子的系数，但对于极短量表而言，这种情况很常见（Tavakol & Dennick, 2011）。

每个 CBI‑P 因子的校正项目‑总分相关均良好（情绪耗竭为>0.60.身体耗竭为>0.50）。项目间相关在0.30–0.70 的可接受范围内（Ferketich，1991），且删除任何项目均不会改善因子信度（见表 S4）。两个CBI‑P 因子之间的因子间相关良好（0.528；表 S5）。

correlations between item 17 (“I’ve had a harder time communicating my point to others”) and item 18 (“I’ve had a harder time finding the right words to communicate what I mean than I usually do”; 0.807), and between item 22 (“I’ve been avoiding social situations, even if I like them, more often than I usually do”) and item 23 (“I’ve wanted to isolate myself from others more often than I usually do”; 0.784). There were weak correlations between item 6 (“I’ve had a harder time controlling my impulses than I usually do”) and item 11 (“I’ve had more, or more severe, meltdowns than I usually do”) with al the avoidance-related items from the Avoidance and Exhaustion factor (items 22, 23, 24 and 25). However, al ABM items were ultimately retained as reliability would not have improved upon their deletion, and the corrected item-total correlations between all items and their respec tive factors were strong (>0.60; see Table S2). Inter-factor correlations between the four ABM factors were good (>0.50) as shown in Table S3.

## Identifying the factor structure of the personal and work scales of the Copenhagen Burnout Inventory

Both KMO (0.857) and Bartlett’s test of sphericity (χ<sup>2</sup> (78) = 1408.69, p < 0.001) confirmed that factor analysis was appropriate for the two CBI scales. Communalities values indicated that all 6 CBI-P items and 7 CBI-W items could be retained for EFA. Inspection of the eigen values suggested a 3-factor solution which was endorsed by parallel analysis, and which explained 64.57% of the variance. Items 1–6 of the CBI-W grouped onto the firs factor, ‘Work-Related Exhaustion’, and the CBI-P items loaded equally onto two factors: factor two, ‘Emotiona Exhaustion’ (items 3, 5, 4), and factor three, ‘Physical Exhaustion’ (items 1, 2, 6). Item 7 of the CBI-W (“Do you have enough energy for family and friends during lei sure time?”) loaded onto Emotional Exhaustion, but the item was discarded as excluding it improved factor reliability from ω = 0.775 to ω = 0.795.

The CBI-W and CBI-P are standalone scales that can be administered and scored separately (Kristensen et al., 2005). In our initial factor analysis comprising both CBI scales, the retained CBI-W items loaded onto the Work-Related Exhaustion factor which accounted for 41.06% of the total variance. To examine whether this underestimated the structure of the CBI-P, EFA was repeated using only the 6 CBI-P items. Parallel analysis and Velicer’s MAP test suggested a single-factor solution (Table S1), however, two factors were ultimately retained (Emotional Exhaustion and Physical Exhaustion) as the variance explained increased from 52.45% to 69.60% with the 2-factor solution. Table 6a shows the factor loadings, ω coefficients and percentage of variance explained for the 3-factor CBI solution, and Table 6b presents the values for the 2-factor CBI-P solution. The reliability coefficients for the 3-factor and 2-factor solutions were slightly lower than for the ABM total and factors, however this is common for very short scales (Tavakol & Dennick, 2011).

The corrected item-total correlations for each CBI-P factor were good (>0.60 for Emotional Exhaustion and >0.50 for Physical Exhaustion). Inter-item correlations were within the acceptable range of 0.30–0.70 (Ferketich, 1991), and factor reliability would not have improved if any items were deleted (see Table S4). The inter-factor correlation between the two CBI-P factors was good (0.528; Table S5).

## Inter-rater reliability

Interpretation of the single measures ICC values andtheir 95% CI (Koo & Li, 2016) are shown in Table 7并表明ABM与CBI‑P总量表（0.517）和CBI‑P‑E（0.545）之间存在中等评分者间信度。ABM与CBI‑W及CBI‑P‑P子量表之间的评分者间信度较差（分别为0.100和0.373）。总体而言，结果表明CBI‑P‑P子量表和CBI‑W不是孤独谱系耗竭的可靠测量工具，而ABM、CBI‑P（总分）和CBI‑P‑E子量表则是中等信度的孤独谱系耗竭测量工具。

## 区分效度

AUC曲线（图1）的比较显示，CBI‑W和CBI‑P的“身体耗竭”子量表（CBI‑P‑P）表现比其他测量工具更差，因此被排除在ROC分析之外。针对ABM、CBI‑P总分以及“情绪耗竭”子量表（CBI‑P‑E）的ROC分析在表8中进行了比较。为了对比，同时纳入了CBI‑P总量表，因为该量表设计为使用总分进行测量（Kristensen等人，2005）。结果表明，在检测参与者的自评孤独谱系耗竭方面，ABM和CBI‑P‑E的表现优于CBI‑P总量表。灵敏度值显示，ABM正确识别了66.7%自评当前正在经历孤独谱系耗竭的参与者（n = 76），而CBI‑P‑E为66.3%。两种测量工具的特异度均良好，自评未经历或不确定是否正在经历孤独谱系耗竭的参与者（n = 87）中，分别有78.1%（ABM）和78.9%（CBI‑P‑E）的得分低于截断值。ABM正确预测了在ABM上得分高于截断值的参与者中78.9%当前正在经历孤独谱系耗竭，而CBI‑P‑E为80.2%。ABM正确预测了得分低于截断值的参与者中65.5%未经历孤独谱系耗竭，而CBI‑P‑E为64.4%。总体而言，在本研究中，ABM和CBI‑P‑E在根据参与者自我报告的孤独谱系耗竭状态进行识别时的准确性相同（均为71.8%）。

## 因子效度

使用AMOS生成了四个探索性模型，以评估通过EFA识别出的ABM和CBI‑P的因子结构与效度（各模型总体概览见图S1）。为改善模型拟合，检查了“协方差”和“回归权重”表中的修正指数，并按期望参数变化值从大到小排序。逐次系统地应用那些逻辑合理且方法学上恰当的修改（Byrne, 2009）。每次修改后，均检查并记录模型拟合统计量（ <sup>2</sup>、SRMR、RMSEA、CFI和TLI），以评估

and indicated moderate inter-rater reliability between the ABM and CBI-P total scale (0.517) and CBI-P-E (0.545). Inter-rater reliability between the ABM and CBI-W and CBI-P-P subscale was poor (0.100 and 0.373, respectively). Overall, the results indicated that the CBI-P-P subscale and CBI-W were not reliable measures of autistic burnout, but that the ABM and CBI-P (total score) and CBI-P-E subscale were moderately reliable measures of autistic burnout.

## Discriminant validity

A comparison of the AUC curves (Figure 1) showed the CBI-W and CBI-P—Physical Exhaustion subscale (CBI-P-P) performed more poorly than the other measures, so they were excluded from ROC analyses. The ROC analyses for the ABM, CBI-P total score, and the Emotional Exhaustion subscale (CBI-P-E) are compared in Table 8. For comparison, the CBI-P total scale was also included as the scale was designed to be measured using a total score (Kristensen et al., 2005). The results showed that the ABM and CBI-P-E outperformed the total CBI-P scale for detecting self-reported autistic burnout among the participants. The sensitivity values showed the ABM correctly identified 66.7% of participants who selfreported they were currently experiencing autistic burnout (n = 76) compared to 66.3% by the CBI-P-E. Specificity was good for both measures, with 78.1% (ABM) and 78.9% (CBI-P-E) of participants who self-reported they were not or were unsure if they were currently experiencing autistic burnout (n = 87) scoring below the cut-off scores. The ABM correctly predicted that 78.9% of participants who scored above the cut-off score on the ABM were currently experiencing autistic burnout, compared to 80.2% by the CBI-P-E. The ABM correctly predicted that 65.5% of the participants who scored below the cut-off score were not experiencing autistic burnout, compared to 64.4% by the CBI-P-E. Overall, the ABM and CBI-P-E were equally accurate (71.8%) at identifying participants based on their self-reported autistic burnout status in the current study.

## Factorial validity

Four exploratory models were generated using AMOS to assess the factorial structure and validity of the ABM and CBI-P identified through EFA (See Figure S1 for a general overview of each model). To improve model fit, modifica tion indices from the ‘Covariances’ and ‘Regression Weights’ tables were examined, and expected paramete change values were sorted from largest to smallest. Changes that were logical and methodologically appropriate were systematically applied, one at a time (Byrne, 2009). Model fit statistics (χ<sup>2</sup>, SRMR, RMSEA, CFI and TLI) were examined and recorded after each alteration to evaluate the

TLI值表明，所有四个探索性模型对数据均为良好拟合，其拟合指数之间的差异极小。为确定最简约的模型，我们随后比较了AIC、BIC和CAIC值（表9）。对于ABM和CBI‑P，最低的BIC和CAIC指数属于单层次因子（或‘高阶因子’）模型，因此代表了数据的最佳拟合。

## 量表维度

## ABM

高阶模型测量了一个总体性的‘孤独谱系耗竭’构念，该构念影响四个低阶因子：‘认知与功能困难’、‘情绪与感觉失调’、‘回避与

TLI values indicated that all four exploratory models were a good fit to the data, with minimal differences among their fit indices. To identify the most parsimonious models, we then compared the AIC, BIC and CAIC values (Table 9). For the ABM and CBI-P, the lowest BIC and CAIC indices belonged to the single hierarchical factor (or ‘higher-order’) models, thus representing the best fit for the data.

## Scale dimensionality

## ABM

The higher-order model measured an overarching ‘Autistic Burnout’ construct which influenced four lower-order factors: ‘Cognitive and Functioning Difficulty’, ‘Emotional and Sensory Dysregulation’, ‘Avoidance and

耗竭’以及‘社交与沟通困难’。高阶因子的分层omega（ω ）值为0.77，表明ABM总分中77%的方差可靠地由‘孤独谱系耗竭’构念测量。解释共同方差（ECV）为0.61，表明61%的共同方差可归因于总体因子‘孤独谱系耗竭’，39%分布在四个低阶因子上。各低阶因子的omega（一般）值为：认知与功能困难 = 0.72，情绪与感觉失调 = 0.56，回避与耗竭 = 0.48，社交与沟通困难=0.48（表10）。结果表明ABM主要为单维度结构，支持使用总分来估计量表信度。

为了更好地理解高阶因子和低阶因子与量表项目之间的关系，我们

将原始4因子EFA的因子载荷与施密德‑莱曼 (S‑L) 变换后的因子载荷进行了比较。 >0.20（表S6）。 >0.20以上的S‑L载荷被保留，且通常低于初始因子载荷，因为它们代表部分相关（Wolff & Preising, 2005）。大多数（27个中的20个）ABM条目的S‑L载荷在一般因子上的值高于在其各自因子上的载荷，而条目18、19、20、21和26在其他因子上存在较低的交叉载荷。结果表明，这些条目可以构成一个简化量表，用于测量一般的“孤独谱系耗竭”构念（Wolff & Preising, 2005）。相比之下，条目7和8更能测量情绪失调因子而非一般的“孤独谱系耗竭”因子，条目22、23和24更能测量回避因子而非一般因子，条目15和16更能测量社交困难因子而非一般因子。该结果支持了先前的发现，

Exhaustion’ and ‘Social and Communication Difficulty’. The omega hierarchical (ω ) value for the higher-order factor was 0.77, which indicated that 77% of the variance in the total ABM score was reliably measured by the ‘Autistic Burnout’ construct. The explained common var iance (ECV) was 0.61 which indicated that 61% of the common variance could be attributed to the general fac tor, ‘Autistic Burnout’ and 39% was spread across the four lower-order factors. The omega (general) values for each lower-order factor were: Cognitive and Functioning Difficulty = 0.72, Emotional and Sensory Dysregulation = 0.56, Avoidance and Exhaustion = 0.48, and Social and Communication Difficulty = 0.48 (Table 10). The results suggested that the ABM was primarily unidi mensional and supported the use of a total score to esti mate scale reliability.

To better understand the relationships between the higher and lower-order factors and scale items, we compared the factor loadings from the original 4-factor EFA with the Schmid-Leiman (S-L) transformed factor loadings. >0.20 (Table S6). S-L loadings >0.20 were retained and were generally lower than the initial facto loadings as they represent part correlations (Wolff & Preising, 2005). The S-L loadings for the majority (20 of 27) ABM items was higher on the general factor than the loading on their individual factors, and items 18, 19, 20, 21, and 26 had low cross-loadings onto other factors. The results suggested the items could form an abbreviated scale to measure a general ‘Autistic Burnout’ construct (Wolff & Preising, 2005). In contrast, items 7 and 8 were better measures of the Emotional Dysregulation factor than the general ‘Autistic Burnout’ factor, items 22, 23 and 24 were better measures of the Avoidance factor than of the general factor, and items 15 and 16 were better measures of the Social Difficulty factor than of the general factor. The results supported the earlier finding

## 即单一般因子、单维度模型最能代表ABM的结构。

## CBI‑P

对于CBI-P. 其上位构念为‘个人耗竭’，它影响两个低阶因子：‘情绪耗竭’和‘身体耗竭’。‘个人耗竭的分层omega (ω )值为0.57，表明CBI‑P总分中方差的57%可靠地由该上位构念测量。ECV为0.54（54%），表明略超过一半的共同可靠方差可归因于一般因子‘个人耗竭’，而剩余的46%分布在两个低阶因子上。特定因子的omega（一般）值分别为：情绪耗竭 = 0.42，身体耗竭=0.45（表10）。结果表明CBI-P具有多维度结构，并为计算子量表分数以评估信度提供了一定支持。

## 我们比较了探索性因子分析载荷和

Schmid‑Leiman转换后的因子载荷的二因子CBI‑P（表S7a）。除了第2项（“你多久感到身体疲惫？”）和第3项（“你多久感到情绪疲惫不堪？”）之外，所有项目的S‑L载荷在一般因子上的值均高于其各自因子，这表明项目1、4、5和6代表了一个总括性的‘个人耗竭构念。然而，由于R建议至少三个因素才能产生可识别的模型，这些结果应谨慎解释。为了克服这一限制，我们在不指定要提取的低阶因子数量的情况下重复了分析，结果见表S7b。一个三因子解是

提出，尽管仅项目1（“您多久感到疲倦？”）负载于名为‘精力’的第三个因子上，且其个体因子载荷低于在一般因子上的载荷（0.48 vs 0.71）。项目2、5和6在一般‘个人耗竭’因子上的载荷也高于其各自因子的载荷。只有项目3（“您多久感到情绪疲惫不堪？”）和项目4（“您多久觉得‘我再也受不了了’？”）的S‑L载荷在其特定因子上更高。 值在该模型下增至0.64（从0.57），表明CBI‑P总分中方差的64%可靠地由涵盖性的‘个人耗竭’构念测量。ECV为0.5（55%），表明一半的共同可靠方差归因于一般因子‘个人耗竭’，其余45%分布在各低阶因子上。对于低阶因子，omega（一般因子）值为：情绪耗竭 = 0.40，身体耗竭 = 0.42，以及精力 = 0.50（表10）。一般因子的解释共同方差几乎保持不变（0.55，而第一模型下为0.54)。这些结果为CBI-P的单层次因子结构和多维度性提供了初步支持。

## 评估ABM和CBI与其他变量之间的关系

## 区分效度

采用斯皮尔曼相关检验ABM、CBI‑P子量表及CBI‑W各项目与其总分，以及

that a single general factor, unidimensional model best represented the structure of the ABM.

## CBI-P

For the CBI-P, the overarching construct was ‘Persona Burnout’ which influenced two lower-order factors named ‘Emotional Exhaustion’ and ‘Physical Exhaus tion’. The omega hierarchical (ω ) value for ‘Persona Burnout’ was 0.57, indicating that 57% of the variance in the CBI-P total score was reliably measured by the over arching construct. The ECV was 0.54 (54%) which indi cated that just over half of the common reliable variance could be attributed to the general factor, ‘Personal Burn out’ and the remaining 46% was spread across the two lower-order factors. The omega (general) values for the specific factors were: Emotional Exhaustion = 0.42 and Physical Exhaustion = 0.45 (Table 10). The results sug gested a multidimensional structure for the CBI-P and some support for calculating subscale scores to assess reliability.

We compared the EFA loadings and Schmid-Leiman transformed factor loadings the 2-factor CBI-P (Table S7a). The S-L loadings for all items except item 2 (“How often are you physically exhausted?”) and item 3 (“How often are you emotionally exhausted?”) were higher on the general factor than their individua factors, which suggested that items 1, 4, 5, and 6 repre sented an overarching ‘Personal Burnout’ construct. However, as R recommends a minimum of three factors to produce an identified model, these results should be interpreted with caution. To overcome this limitation, the analysis was repeated without specifying the number of lower-order factors to be extracted and the results are presented in Table S7b. A 3-factor solution was proposed, although only item 1 (“How often do you feel tired?”) loaded onto the third factor named ‘Energy’ and its individual factor loading was lower than its loading on the general factor (0.48 compared to 0.71). Items 2, 5, and 6 also had a higher loading on the general ‘Persona Burnout’ factor than their individual factors. Only the S-L loadings for item 3 (“How often are you emotionally exhausted?”) and item 4 (“How often do you think ‘I can’t take it anymore’?”) were higher on their specific factor. The ω value increased to 0.64 (from 0.57) under this model indicating that 64% of the variance in the total CBI-P score was reliably measured by the overarching ‘Personal Burnout’ construct. The ECV was 0.5 (55%) which indicated that half of the common reliable variance was attributed to the general factor, ‘Personal Burnout and the remaining 45% was spread across the three lower-order factors. For the lower-order factors, the omega (general) values were: Emotional Exhaustion = 0.40, Physical Exhaustion = 0.42, and Energy = 0.50 (Table 10). The explained common variance of the general factor remained almost identical (0.55 compared to 0.54 under the first model). The results provided some preliminary support for a single hierarchical factor structure and multidimensionality of the CBI-P.

PHQ‑9总分（表S8）在项目层面的区分效度。区分效度得到支持，因为各倦怠测量中的每个项目与其各自问卷总分的相关性均高于与PHQ‑9总分的相关性。

ROC分析检验了量表层面的区分效度，并比较了PHQ‑9、GAD‑7、DASS‑S和FFS在当前样本中检测自评孤独谱系耗竭的准确度。AUC曲线比较（图2）显示，PHQ‑9的AUC略高于其他测量工具。DASS‑S报告的灵敏度最高（63.74%），GAD‑7报告的特异度最高（76%）。总体检验准确度以DASS‑S最高（68.71%），但所有测量工具的表现相近（表11）。在参与者中，没有一项精神压力和疲劳测量在检测自评孤独谱系耗竭方面比ABM和CBI‑P‑E更准确（表7）。

## 聚合效度与区分效度

研究参与者报告了较高的抑郁、焦虑、压力和疲劳水平，以及较低的生活满意度（表2）。表12显示了ABM和CBI-P（总分和因素）与抑郁、焦虑、压力、疲劳和掩饰测量指标，以及幸福感（生活满意度、社会融合和社会贡献）测量指标之间的斯皮尔曼相关性和95%置信区间。ABM和CBI‑P（总分和因素）与精神压力（抑郁、焦虑和压力）测量指标呈现强而显著的正相关。疲劳与ABM（总分和因素）呈中等程度相关，但与CBI‑P总分和子量表之间呈现强正相关

（rs在0.61至0.72之间）。ABM与CBI‑P之间以及ABM与CBI‑P‑E之间存在强相关性（分别为r = 0.48和$\Upsilon _ { s } = 0 . 4 9 )$ ），表明具有收敛效度，而ABM与CBI‑P‑P之间的相关性为中等（r = 0.34）。尽管伪装一直被确认为孤独谱系耗竭的突出风险因素（Higgins et al.,2021；Mantzalas et al., 2021；Raymaker et al.,2020），但CAT‑Q与ABM和CBI‑P之间的相关性为中等（分别为r = 0.36和r = 0.30），并且在因素层面，大多数相关性较弱。虽然大多数相关性具有统计学显著性，但CAT‑Q与精神压力、疲劳和幸福感变量之间的相关性较弱，且未达到r = 0.30的阈值。CAT‑Q与社会贡献之间的相关性可忽略不计。请注意：此处提供了CBI‑P（总分和子量表）的结果以作完整性参考，但由于量表维度问题尚未解决，使用CBI‑P总分的结果应谨慎解读。

使用Fisher r到z变换进一步评估了耗竭测量工具与抑郁、焦虑、压力和疲劳之间正相关的幅度（表S9）。ABM、CBI‑P（总分及子量表）与PHQ‑9之间的相关比较均具有统计学显著性（分别为p = 0.01、p = 0.02和p ≤ 0.001）。结果表明ABM与PHQ‑9之间存在聚合效度，提示在该样本中孤独谱系耗竭和抑郁量表测量的是相似构念。孤独谱系耗竭测量与焦虑和压力测量之间亦显示出聚合效度。结果显示，ABM与GAD‑7（p = 0.01，与ABM—CBI‑P‑P相比）以及ABM与DASS‑S（p = 0.04，与ABM—CBI‑P相比）之间存在统计学显著的相关。

well as the total score of the PHQ-9 (Table S8). Discrimi nant validity was supported as all items on each of the burnout measure correlated more strongly with the tota score of their respective questionnaire than with the total score of the PHQ-9.

ROC analyses examined scale-level discriminant validity and compared how accurately the PHQ-9, GAD-7, DASS-S, and FFS detected self-reported autistic burnout in the current sample. Comparison of the AUC curves (Figure 2) showed the AUC for the PHQ-9 was slightly higher than for the other measures. The DASS-S reported the highest sensitivity (63.74%) and the GAD-7 reported highest specificity (76%). Overall test accuracy was highest for the DASS-S (68.71%), but all measures performed similarly (Table 11). None of the mental strain and fatigue measures were more accurate than the ABM and CBI-P-E at detecting self-reported autistic burnou among the participants (Table 7).

## Convergent and divergent validity

Elevated rates of depression, anxiety, stress, and fatigue, and lower life satisfaction were reported by study participants (Table 2). Table 12 shows the Spearman correla tions and 95% CI between the ABM and CBI-P (totals and factors) with measures of depression, anxiety, stress, fatigue, and camouflaging, and measures of wellbeing (satisfaction with life, social integration and social contri bution). The ABM and CBI-P (totals and factors) showed strong, significant positive correlations with mea sures of mental strain (depression, anxiety, and stress). Fatigue was moderately correlated with the ABM (tota and factors), but there were strong, positive correlations between fatigue and the CBI-P total and subscales (r between 0.61 and 0.72). There were strong correlations between the ABM and CBI-P and the ABM and CBI-$\mathrm { P - E } \left( r _ { s } = 0 . 4 8 \mathrm { a n d } r _ { s } = 0 . 4 9 \right.$ respectively), suggesting convergent validity, while the correlation between the ABM and the CBI-P-P was moderate (r<sub>s</sub> = 0.34). Although masking has been consistently identified as a prominent risk factor for autistic burnout (Higgins et al., 2021; Mantzalas et al., 2021; Raymaker et al., 2020), the corre lations between the CAT-Q and the ABM and CBI-P were moderate (r = 0.36 and r = 0.30, respectively), and at the factor level, most correlations were weak. While most correlations were statistically significant, the correlations between the CAT-Q and mental strain, fatigue and wellbeing variables were weak and did no reach the r = 0.30 threshold. The correlation between the CAT-Q and social contribution was negligible. Please note: Results using CBI-P (total and subscales) have been provided for completeness, but results using the CBI-P total score should be interpreted with caution due to unresolved questions about scale dimensionality.

The magnitude of the positive correlations between the burnout measures and depression, anxiety, stress, and fatigue were assessed further using Fisher’s r to z transformations (Table S9). Correlation comparisons among the ABM, CBI-P (total and subscales) and PHQ-9 were all statistically significant ( p = 0.01, p = 0.02, and p ≤ 0.001, respectively). The results indicated convergent validity between the ABM and PHQ-9 and suggested the autistic burnout and depression scales were measuring a similar construct in this sample. Convergent validity was also suggested between the autistic burnout measure and measures of anxiety and stress. The results showed statistically significant correlations 相比之下，ABM、CBI‑P‑E与FFS之间的相关比较表明 ABM与FFS之间存在区分效度 $( \mathtt { p } = 0 . 0 5 )$ ，提示在 该样本中ABM与FFS测量的是不同构念。
